| AC-233 | the **colour presets** | `packages/excalidraw/tests/flowColorPresets.test.tsx` | The Look section offers the eight presets; one sets the shape, text and animation colours and the glow together and leaves the arrow, the target and the speed alone; the variant for the page's background is the one applied; every colour of every preset is at least 3 : 1 against the page it is for, computed with the WCAG contrast formula; the shape colour and the animation colour of a preset differ in hue by at least 60 degrees; the chosen preset shows as chosen; a preset writes through to a marked chart; and Classic clears the colours and the glow. Tests: `AC-233 the Look section offers the presets`, `AC-233 a preset sets the colours and the glow together`, `AC-233 a preset leaves the arrow, the target and the speed alone`, `AC-233 the variant for the page background is applied`, `AC-233 every colour reads on its page at 3 to 1 or better`, `AC-233 the shape and animation colours differ in hue`, `AC-233 the chosen preset shows as chosen`, `AC-233 a preset writes through to a marked chart`, `AC-233 classic clears the colours and the glow`. |
| AC-234 | **glow**: the highlight as a **neon tube** | `packages/excalidraw/tests/flowGlow.test.tsx` | Glow is off by default; the switch in the Look section turns it on; with glow on a highlight has a bright core (its colour lightened toward white), a halo of several shadows in its colour and **no fill**; the glow is in the colour of what it surrounds - the shape's colour round an outline, the text colour round a text box and letters, the animation colour round what travels; it reaches the class entry and the presenter's snapshot; and an entry from before it has none. Tests: `AC-234 glow is off by default`, `AC-234 the switch turns it on`, `AC-234 a glowing highlight has a bright core and a halo and no fill`, `AC-234 the glow is in the colour of what it surrounds`, `AC-234 glow reaches the class entry and the presenter snapshot`, `AC-234 an entry from before has no glow`. |
| AC-235 | the **shape** and the **text** are coloured apart | `packages/excalidraw/tests/flowGlow.test.tsx` | The Look section has a Shape pad and a Text pad; the shape takes the shape colour; the text box and the letters take the text colour; the text colour follows the shape colour until it is set; it is validated, remembered, written to the class entry and sent to the presenter; and an entry from before it follows the shape. Tests: `AC-235 the text colour is set apart from the shape colour`, `AC-235 the shape takes the shape colour`, `AC-235 the box and the letters take the text colour`, `AC-235 the text colour follows the shape colour until it is set`, `AC-235 it is remembered, saved and sent`, `AC-235 an entry from before follows the shape`. |
| AC-236 | the **pipe** takes the animation colour | `packages/excalidraw/tests/flowGlow.test.tsx` | With an animation colour set, the pipe's strokes are made from it - a darker shade, the colour, a lighter tint - and they are computed, so any colour has a pipe; with none set the pipe is dark, grey and white as it was; and the pipe glows when glow is on. Tests: `AC-236 the pipe is made from the animation colour`, `AC-236 the shades are computed from the colour`, `AC-236 with no colour the pipe is as it was`, `AC-236 the pipe glows when glow is on`. |
| AC-237 | what the overlay draws **lines up** with the canvas | `packages/excalidraw/tests/flowLabels.test.tsx` | The letters and the arrow labels are SVG text with the canvas's own baseline offsets for each line; a text box or letters highlight is lifted about its own centre and not the node's; and a node's outline is inside its box. Tests: `AC-237 the letters are drawn at the canvas's baselines`, `AC-237 arrow labels use the same rule`, `AC-237 a text highlight lifts about its own centre`, `AC-237 the outline is inside the node's box`. |
| AC-238 | a change of the **view alone** sends the view alone | `excalidraw-app/tests/presenterViewOnly.test.tsx` | A scroll, a zoom or a camera flight sends a camera message and not the scene; the scene goes when elements change and when a presenter connects; and the presenter moves only its view for a camera message. Tests: `AC-238 a view change alone sends the view alone`, `AC-238 the scene is not sent for a view change`, `AC-238 the presenter moves only its view`, `AC-238 the scene goes when elements change`, `AC-238 the scene goes when a presenter connects`. |
| AC-239 | a label is **never seen twice** | `packages/excalidraw/tests/flowLabels.test.tsx` | While the overlay draws a label again, the canvas is given the list without that label; the canvas draws it again when the overlay stops; moving the chart keeps one set of labels; the document and an exported image keep the label; and the presenter does the same. Tests: `AC-239 the canvas does not draw a label the overlay draws again`, `AC-239 the canvas draws it again when the overlay stops`, `AC-239 moving the chart keeps one set of labels`, `AC-239 the document and an exported image keep the label`, `AC-239 the presenter does the same`. |
| AC-240 | leaving the animation is **clear** | `packages/excalidraw/tests/flowBarPill.test.tsx` | The pill says what is running and has a Stop button; Stop on the pill ends the animation without opening the bar; Minimise only collapses and the animation goes on; Stop switches the preview off; Esc exits whatever is running in one press; and Esc leaves other modes and text editing alone. Tests: `AC-240 the pill says what is running and has a Stop button`, `AC-240 Stop on the pill ends the animation`, `AC-240 Minimise only collapses and the animation goes on`, `AC-240 Stop switches the preview off`, `AC-240 Esc exits whatever is running`, `AC-240 Esc leaves other modes and text editing alone`. |
| AC-241 | the **laser pointer** is a neon tube | `packages/excalidraw/tests/laserNeon.test.tsx` | The laser trail is drawn as a halo path in the laser's colour with layered shadows and a thinner core path in that colour lightened toward white; a collaborator's laser is the same in its own colour; the trail still fades and keeps its length; and no other trail is changed. Tests: `AC-241 the laser trail has a bright core and a halo`, `AC-241 the core is the colour lightened toward white`, `AC-241 the halo has layered shadows in the laser colour`, `AC-241 a collaborator's laser is neon in its own colour`, `AC-241 the trail still fades`, `AC-241 other trails are unchanged`. |
| AC-242 | the **emoji bar** has the toolbox's move and close | `excalidraw-app/tests/stickerDock.test.tsx` | The emoji bar is a dock with a move grip and a close button; dragging the grip moves it and it snaps to an edge; close turns it into a pill and the pill brings it back; its place is remembered; and its tabs, its search and its drag onto the canvas still work. Tests: `AC-242 the emoji bar is a dock with a move grip and a close button`, `AC-242 dragging the grip moves it and it snaps to an edge`, `AC-242 close turns it into a pill and the pill brings it back`, `AC-242 its place is remembered`, `AC-242 its tabs and its search still work`. |
